The function that rotates a mesh converted its angle the wrong way (`radToDeg` instead of `degToRad`). Any turned mesh node (a \"facedir\", \"wallmounted\" or \"degrotate\" rotation) came out at a meaningless angle. The drawing code had the front and back directions swapped, unlike the rest of the engine and the Lua API. If you can provide the video, I'll check each object against it.\n\n### Fixes\n1. **Mesh nodes are rotated by the wrong angle** (`src/client/mesh.cpp`). The function that rotates a mesh converted its angle the wrong way (`radToDeg` instead of `degToRad`). Any turned mesh node (a \"facedir\", \"wallmounted\" or \"degrotate\" rotation) came out at a meaningless angle. This affected every rotated mesh, not just certain spots.\n\n2. **Resized box-shaped nodes drift away from their position** (`src/client/content_mapblock.cpp`, `drawAutoLightedCuboid`). The node's world position was added *before* its `visual_scale` was applied, so the position was scaled too. The further a node is from the world origin, the further off it's drawn. The fix scales the shape first, then moves it into place.\n\n3. **Connected nodes (fences, walls, panes) attach on the wrong side** (`content_mapblock.cpp`). The drawing code had the front and back directions swapped, unlike the rest of the engine and the Lua API. Connections toward a north or south neighbour were drawn on the opposite side.\n\n4. **Connected nodes won't join a rotated neighbour** (`src/nodedef.cpp`, `nodeboxConnects`). The connection-direction rotation was applied even when the neighbour doesn't restrict which sides it connects on, and that check always failed. So a fence wouldn't connect to an ordinary rotated node (stairs, a chest) placed next to it. It now does that rotation only when the neighbour does restrict its sides.\n\nI also fixed one smaller case in `drawMeshNode`: with both mesh caching and smooth lighting turned on, wallmounted meshes were rotated a second time with the wrong value. They now reuse the already-rotated cached copy.\n\n### What I checked and found correct\n- I tested the full table that rotates a turned cube's faces and textures with a script, against the same rotation rules the code uses for shapes and meshes. All 24 orientations × 6 faces matched.\n- Full cubes and other boxes put the same texture rotation on every face.\n- Torches, signs, wall-mounted node boxes, plants (including rooted and degree-rotated ones), and the placement helpers (`dir_to_facedir`, `dir_to_wallmounted`, `rotate_and_place`) are correct.\n\nNothing is committed; the changes are in the working tree.","ttft_ms":5374,"type":"result","duration_ms":250152,"uuid":"b756253b-17a0-4ab0-886f-22e0c3f482d7","ttft_stream_ms":3982,"time_to_request_ms":193,"first_content_frame_ms":4023,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0,"model_usage_since_last_result":{"anthropic/claude-opus-5-5":{"inputTokens":90,"outputTokens":22713,"cacheReadInputTokens":2584193,"cacheCreationInputTokens":77945,"webSearchRequests":0,"costUSD":1.3611836000000002,"contextWindow":1000000,"maxOutputTokens":128000,"thinkingTokens":12375,"cacheCreationEphemeral5mInputTokens":77945,"cacheCreationEphemeral1hInputTokens":0,"canonicalModel":"claude-opus-5-5","provider":"firstParty","costBasis":"list"}}}
